The funding, the settlement and the refund claim all submit through it (libspiffy-m715). Also: `TransactionStatusMessage.status` is ARC's wire name (`SEEN_ON_NETWORK`), as every other status the wallet handles, instead of a lowercase vocabulary (`seen_on_network`, `double_spend`) nothing else used; the private `_arcStatusToString` is deleted and its log lines print the wire name. | `payment_channel_manager_rejection_test.dart`: a funding answered in flight and then held opens (checks followed, same txid); in flight then contested does not; in flight past the bound does not, and ARC was followed. `payment_channel_manager_settlement_test.dart`: the same for a settlement. `RecordingArcActor` answers status checks (`laterStatuses`). Mutations: no follow (5 red), the check's answer ignored (4), ACCEPTED_BY_NETWORK not in flight (2), STORED not in flight (3), no bound (5). Localnet: the channel scenarios that failed with it |
| V-151 | High | A payment handed over a second time was refused, telling the payer the payment it made had failed | `spv_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet payment run (22 Sep). A payer that hears no answer hands its payment over again. The second `ValidateBEEFCommand` for the same transaction and invoice was answered `valid: false`, "Invoice ... is not pending (status: paid)": the invoice the payment itself had paid. **Fix:** `_validateInvoicePayment` takes the txid; an invoice paid by this same transaction accepts it as the payment it already is (`alreadyPaid`), checks it as the first delivery was checked, and does not mark the invoice paid again. Another transaction for a paid invoice is still refused (libspiffy-6142). | `received_payment_is_submitted_test.dart`: the same payment delivered twice is answered valid both times, the balance counts it once, the invoice journal has one `invoice.paid`; a different payment for the paid invoice is refused 'is not pending'. Mutations: never already paid (1 red), any txid counts (1). Not killed: marking the invoice paid again, whose only trace is eventador's stdout print of the aggregate's refusal; libspiffy-mu09 makes that refusal visible |
| V-152 | Critical | A node restarted at the chain tip never synced another header | `spiffynode_bridge.dart`, `header_sync_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet payment run (22 Sep): Bob, restarted between receiving a payment and its block, never confirmed it. A restarted node whose stored headers reach the tip asks for headers at startup and is answered with an empty `headers` message. `LibSpiffyPeerHandler.handleHeaders` forwarded only non-empty batches, so `HeaderSyncActor._syncInProgress`, set when the request went out, was never cleared, and every later sync (each new block announced) was skipped at FINE level: the node stopped following the chain for good, and nothing it received or sent was confirmed again. A fresh node escaped it only because its first answers are full batches. The flag also never expired, so any getHeaders whose answer never came (a peer dropping mid-request) wedged the sync the same way. **Fix:** an empty answer is forwarded and ends the request; the in-flight request is a timestamp (`_syncRequestedAt`) that expires after `syncRequestTimeout` (new `HeaderSyncActor` parameter, default 30 s), with a warning, so the next trigger asks again (libspiffy-3pyc). | `spiffynode_bridge_integration_test.dart`: an empty headers answer reaches HeaderSyncActor (the two forwarding tests beside it asserted nothing and now check what arrived). `header_sync_actor_test.dart`: a request in flight holds off another until its answer, even an empty one; an unanswered request expires and the next sync asks again. Mutations: empty answer dropped (1 red), never expires (1), never holds (2), request not marked (2). Localnet: `localnet_node_e2e_test.dart`, a node restarted at the tip follows the next two blocks (timed out on the old code) |
| V-153 | High | An app never heard its payment confirm or its invoice paid, and a refused mark-paid went unnoticed | `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart`, `libspiffy_actor_system.dart`, `spv_actor.dart`, `invoice_aggregate.dart`, `invoice_coordinator_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet payment run (22 Sep): Bob's wallet confirmed the payment (ARC MINED, proof checked against the header), and the test waiting for `TransactionConfirmedEvent` timed out. The coordinator's `TransactionConfirmedEvent` and `InvoicePaidEvent` are public and documented, and nothing in the library constructed either; the core events of the same names hid that from a search. SPVActor told the invoice coordinator `MarkInvoicePaidMessage` and ignored the answer, and the invoice aggregate answered a refused command `success: true`, status `pending`, whatever the invoice's state, so a refusal (an invoice paid or expired between the check and the mark) left the invoice as it was while the payment was answered valid, with only eventador's stdout print. **Fix:** the wallet and invoice ProjectionActors' `appliedEvents` feed the coordinator (`readModelEvents`, as channel events already are), which announces a core `TransactionConfirmedEvent` with a height as the coordinator's `TransactionConfirmedEvent` and an `InvoicePaidEvent` as its own: after the read model holds them. The aggregate answers a refusal `success: false` with the error and the invoice's actual status; the invoice coordinator checks `success`; SPVActor logs a refusal naming the invoice (libspiffy-mu09). Events nothing emits at all (`BalanceUpdatedEvent`, `UTXOSplitStartedEvent`, `HeaderSyncProgressEvent`, `subscribeToWalletEvents`) are filed for the owner (libspiffy-7ye4). | `arc_proof_journal_rebuild_test.dart`: an ARC confirmation is announced once, with wallet, txid and height, and the read model shows it confirmed when it is. `received_payment_is_submitted_test.dart`: the paid invoice is announced once with invoice, txid and amount; a redelivered payment logs no refusal. Mutations: confirmation not announced (2 red), paid invoice not announced (1), stream not wired (2), invoice marked paid again (1, the V-151 mutation that survived) |
| V-154 | Medium | A received payment was answered as on the network before its outputs were spendable in the read model | `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet payment run (22 Sep): Bob's answer said `broadcasted`, `SEEN_ON_NETWORK`, and a balance query made on it showed nothing received. A received output is pending until the network holds the payment; ARCActor tells the wallet `MarkUTXOAvailableCommand` as it answers the submission, and `_answerPayment` emitted the answer at once, about 300 ms before the projection applied it. xggs made the answer wait for the payment row; the outputs were not waited for. **Fix:** on a held answer (`SEEN_ON_NETWORK` or `MINED`) `_answerPayment` waits for the wallet projection to apply `UTXOMarkedAvailableEvent` for each received output, or finds it already available, before emitting; a wait that fails is logged and the answer still goes out (libspiffy-vj4j). | `received_payment_is_submitted_test.dart`: the received output is `available` and the balance holds it when the answer arrives. Mutation: no wait (the output is still `pending`, red 3 of 3). Localnet: `localnet_payment_e2e_test.dart`, all four scenarios (invoice paid, submitted by the receiver, mined, both wallets confirmed; a spend of an unmined receive; a payment handed over twice; a restart between receipt and block) |
| V-155 | High | A deferred payment's answer came before the read model held it, so cancelling, reclaiming or listing it at once was told it was no deferred payment | `payment_coordinator_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet deferred-payment run (22 Sep): Alice cancelled the payment she had just handed Bob, and was answered "Transaction ... is not a deferred payment of wallet ..."; a reclaim and a listing made on the answer failed the same way. A payment is recorded with a deferred spend: the wallet journals the transaction, then the hold (`TransactionSpendDeferredEvent`) that keeps its inputs. `_recordOutgoingTransaction` waited for the projection to apply the transaction and the payment was answered, while the hold was still on its way to the read model. The mocked test polled for the row after every payment. **Fix:** a deferred recording waits for its own hold, which the wallet always journals last (and alone when a cancelled payment is recorded again); any other recording waits for its transaction as before. The provisioning split, its earmarks and channel funding record the same way and get the same guarantee (libspiffy-3qnx). | `deferred_payment_coordinator_test.dart`: the payment's answer finds it outstanding in the read model, its input held by it, and it cancels at once; the poll after every payment is removed. Mutation: the old wait (the new test and the two re-payment tests red, 3 of 3). Localnet: `localnet_deferred_e2e_test.dart` |
| V-156 | High | Checking, broadcasting or reclaiming a deferred payment was answered before the read model showed what the network said | `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart` | Found by the localnet deferred-payment run (22 Sep): ARC answered a check `NOT_FOUND` and the payment listed right after had no status; a broadcast answered `SEEN_ON_NETWORK` while its input still showed held and the payment outstanding; a reclaim answered before the payment was reclaimed and the coins came back. ARCActor tells the wallet the status, the spend and the change before it answers, and the coordinator emitted the answer at once. Cancelling already waited for the read model; checking, broadcasting and reclaiming did not, and the mocked tests polled after each. **Fix:** `_awaitNetworkAnswerApplied` waits, before any of the three answers, for the status checked since the request (`TransactionNetworkStatusCheckedEvent`), a rejected payment's failure, and, when the network has the transaction, the wallet's inputs to it spent and its outputs to the wallet available; a wait that fails is reported in the answer's `error` and logged (libspiffy-kuqv). | `deferred_payment_coordinator_test.dart`: every answer is followed by read-model assertions without polling (status recorded, input spent and payment seen, contested status recorded, rejected payment failed and input released, reclaim resolved and its output spendable). Mutation: no wait (7 red, 3 of 3). Localnet: `localnet_deferred_e2e_test.dart` |
| V-157 | High | A reclaim or a deferred broadcast ARC answered in flight was reported as succeeded, and one in the orphan mempool too | `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart`, `coordinator_messages.dart` | Found by the localnet deferred-payment run (22 Sep): Bob had broadcast his copy of Alice's payment, and Alice's reclaim of it was answered with an in-flight status (ARC stops waiting for the network after five seconds and answers with where it got to), reported `success: true`; ARC's next status scan found the reclaim REJECTED. V-150 follows an in-flight answer for channel submissions; the deferred broadcast and the reclaim took any answer but REJECTED and DOUBLE_SPEND_ATTEMPTED as success, SEEN_IN_ORPHAN_MEMPOOL included (an input unknown or already spent in a block). **Fix:** `_followInFlight` asks ARC again (`CheckDeferredPaymentStatusMessage`, recorded like any explicit check) every second while the answer is in flight, for up to 30 s; the broadcast and the reclaim succeed only when the network holds the transaction (`SEEN_ON_NETWORK`, `MINED`), and `_notHeldReason` says why otherwise (still in flight, orphan mempool, contested, rejected). The event docs and the README section on deferred payments say so; the README no longer claims DOUBLE_SPEND_ATTEMPTED fails a payment, and documents the reclaim (libspiffy-ggsg). | `deferred_payment_coordinator_test.dart`: a broadcast answered in flight is followed to SEEN_ON_NETWORK and the payment is seen; a reclaim answered in flight after the recipient's copy reached the network is rejected, names the copy, and the payment is not reclaimed; a broadcast in the orphan mempool is unsuccessful and the payment stays held. `NetworkArc.answerInFlight` answers ACCEPTED_BY_NETWORK and gives its verdict to status queries, as ARC does. Mutations: no follow (2 red, 3 of 3), the old success rule (1). Localnet: `localnet_deferred_e2e_test.dart`, all five scenarios (the payer broadcasts a payment the payee never did; cancels one, and the payee's later copy is recorded paid; reclaims one, and the payee's copy is a double spend; loses the race to the payee's copy; the payee's copy after the reclaim is mined) |
| V-158 | Medium | A failed payment was answered before its inputs were released, so a retry on hearing it found them still reserved | `payment_coordinator_actor.dart`, `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart`, `projection_barrier.dart` (new) | Found while tightening the deferred-payment tests for V-156 (22 Sep): paying an invoice again after the network rejected the first payment was answered as failed with the input still `reserved` in the read model; the test polled until it came back. `_handlePayInvoice` reported every failure after reserving (eight sites in `_payWithReservedUtxos`, and its unexpected errors) at once, and only then, in `finally`, told the wallet to cancel the deferred spends it had recorded and release the reservation. **Fix:** `_payWithReservedUtxos` reports a failure through `fail`; the caller releases, waits until the read model shows none of the selected inputs reserved by the payment or its deferred spends (`_awaitReleased`: a release, a cancellation, or the row already free; a release with nothing left to release journals nothing), and then answers. The barrier the coordinator used for this (`_awaitProjectionApplied`) moves to `awaitProjectionApplied` in `projection_barrier.dart`, shared by both actors instead of copied (libspiffy-64un). | `deferred_payment_coordinator_test.dart`: the failed re-payment finds its input `available` when the failure arrives, without polling. Mutation: no wait (red 3 of 3). `payment_coordinator_reservation_test.dart` and `payment_coordinator_plugin_guard_test.dart` give their fakes what a read model has (the projection answers the barrier, storage returns the UTXO row); `await_event_applied_timeout_guard_test.dart` scans `projection_barrier.dart` |
| V-159 | High | An invoice was marked paid by a payment the network refuses, and a paid invoice then turned the payer's genuine replacement away | `spv_actor.dart`, `wallet_coordinator_actor.dart`, `wallet_messages.dart` | Found by the localnet deferred-payment run (22 Sep) and settled by the owner (23 Sep): an invoice is paid as early as ARC's `SEEN_ON_NETWORK`, and the payment is still followed to its block, which is where the merkle proof of our own outputs comes from. SPVActor marked the invoice paid when the BEEF validated — before the payment was submitted, so also for the copy ARC answered `DOUBLE_SPEND_ATTEMPTED` (the payer had reclaimed first) and for one spending an output already spent in a block (`SEEN_IN_ORPHAN_MEMPOOL`). A valid BEEF says the funding history is anchored in blocks, never that the network took this payment; and since V-151 a paid invoice refuses every other transaction, so the payer's replacement was refused 'is not pending'. **Fix:** SPVActor reports what the payment pays the invoice (`SPVValidationResult.invoicePaidAmount` / `invoicePaidAddresses`) instead of marking it; `_answerPayment` marks it paid when ARC answers that the network holds it (`SEEN_ON_NETWORK`/`MINED`), or when the payment arrived with its own verified proof, and waits for the invoice aggregate's answer, which the receive answer reports. An answer ARC gave while still taking the payment to the network is no verdict and pays nothing yet. ARC follows every one of them to its block regardless (a contested payment stays `broadcast`, an orphan `orphaned`, both scanned), and `_payInvoiceOfHeldPayment` marks the invoice paid when the read model shows the network holding the payment after all — its outputs made available (V-154) or a verified proof confirming it — including after a restart, matching the payment to the invoice by its own single-use addresses, which it must cover. The SPVActor branch that logged a refused mark-paid (V-153) is deleted with the tell it answered (libspiffy-yyby). | `received_payment_is_submitted_test.dart`: a payment ARC answers DOUBLE_SPEND_ATTEMPTED, and one it answers SEEN_IN_ORPHAN_MEMPOOL, leave the invoice pending with no `invoice.paid` journalled and no announcement, and the payer's replacement pays it; an answer given in flight pays nothing yet; a payment carrying its own verified proof pays its invoice. `invoice_paid_when_the_network_holds_the_payment_test.dart` (new): the invoice settles when the outputs become available or a block confirms the payment, for what it paid; a payment that does not cover it, one paying nobody's invoice, and an invoice that is not pending are left alone. Mutations: marked at validation (2 red, 3 of 3), paid on any ARC answer (2), no settlement when held (1), none when confirmed (1), any amount (2), any invoice (3). `spv_receive_output_reading_test.dart` and `full_spv_validation_test.dart` assert the new rule; the frozen-collection guard is 140 |
| V-160 | Medium | Three integration test files had diverged from the library and tested nothing they claimed | `cdn_header_sync_integration_test.dart`, `header_sync_e2e_test.dart`, `spiffynode_integration_test.dart`, `node_rpc_data_source_test.dart`, `node_rpc_wif_import_test.dart` | Found when the session's suite list was rebuilt (23 Sep) after the scratchpad was cleared: these files had been dropped from it and were failing at HEAD, so nothing covered them. **CDN:** the chain was built with `skipProofOfWorkValidation: true`, which also skips seeding the genesis anchor (`BlockHeaderChain.initialize`), so the chain had no tip and the service refused the first chunk for not starting at genesis (SPV-02's anchoring rule, correctly). The fixtures are real testnet headers from height 1: the chain is now built as production builds it, `params: NetworkParams.testnet`, and the chunks link to the seeded genesis. **Header sync e2e:** the fixtures are the first mainnet blocks and the system defaulted to testnet, so every header was rejected with an unknown parent; it initializes with `networkType: 'main'`. Its storage-error test stored into healthy storage and asserted the bridge returned true (its own comment said so): the storage now really fails the write, nothing is counted that was not written, and the headers land when it recovers. Its merkle-proof test asserted `isA<bool>()`, which every answer satisfies: it asserts the verdict. The hollow 'actor communication failures' test (a duplicate of the storage one, with a comment saying it simulated no failure) is deleted; the bridge's failure paths are covered by `spiffynode_bridge_integration_test`. **SpiffyNode integration:** its 'realistic header sync performance' test stored five separate twenty-header chains at caller-chosen heights 0..99 and expected height 99; heights come from the parent now, so only the first batch linked. It is one chain of 100 delivered in batches, with the machine-dependent 5 s bound dropped. Five tests that only checked a message constructor kept its arguments are deleted (one of them kept the dead duplicate `ValidateBEEFMessage` alive), and the merkle-proof and sync-progress assertions are real. **Node RPC:** both files pointed at a hard-coded LAN address (`192.168.50.241:18332`) while their own prerequisites said `localhost:18332`, and their reachability guard did not stop them failing; they point at the localnet node and are tagged `localnet`, skipped unless asked for, like every other test that needs the stack (libspiffy-rto8). | The files themselves: CDN 9, header sync 8, SpiffyNode 6, all passing; the node RPC files skip without the stack. Suite list rebuilt: all `*_test.dart` minus `localnet_`, `/postgres/`, `type42` |
| V-161 | Low | Two SPV message classes existed twice, and three more nothing ever sent | `spv_messages.dart`, `spv_actor.dart` | Queued cleanup, unblocked by V-160 (the one test that kept a duplicate alive only checked that its constructor stored its arguments). `spv_messages.dart` defined a second `ValidateBEEFMessage` and `RetrieveMerkleProofMessage` beside the live ones in `wallet_messages.dart`; nothing sent or handled the copies, and SPVActor imported `spv_messages.dart` with `hide ValidateBEEFMessage` plus a second aliased import (`as wmsg show RetrieveMerkleProofMessage`) to tell them apart — duct tape over one type too many. `SPVControlMessage`, `SPVControlAction` and `SPVConfigMessage` had no sender or handler anywhere in the library. **Fix:** all five are deleted and SPVActor imports each message file plainly. None was exported from `libspiffy.dart`, so no public API changes. | The suite: 2,825 passing with the deletions; the frozen-collection guard goes back to 139 with `SPVControlMessage.parameters` gone |

Also made testable without behaviour change: `CdnHeaderSyncService.cacheFilePath`
(`@visibleForTesting`), `LibSpiffyActorSystem.arcConfig` getter,
`PostgresEventStore.beforeReplayQuery` / `afterReplayQuery` test hooks.
